## 2019

### Monday, July 8, 2019

[Meeting page](../../meetings/2019/2019-07-08.md) · [Full transcript](../../transcripts/2019/2019-07-08.md) · [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IlL1lamK-Bk) · City of South Lyon Council Meeting July 8 2019

[0:19:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IlL1lamK-Bk&t=1174s) Good evening. So during our transition process, we set up a committee for awards. And what we have done is we've had three sergeants that will give out certificates that officers go above and beyond the call of duty Or do something very well or they present themselves professionally or in a professional manner. The sergeants are Sergeant Searland, Sergeant Fott, and Sergeant Whitrock. In this particular case, and what we're going to be warning them today, Is a certificate of merit through the department on their actions on May 20th of this year.

[0:20:19](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IlL1lamK-Bk&t=1219s) read right here because I want to make sure I get it right. On May 20th, at approximately 9 .44 p .m., Sergeant Mike Wigrock and Officer Ron Barber were dispatched to the South Lion Woods Manufactured Home Park in response to a suicidal subject in the 200 block of Longfellow Drive. The subject admitted to drinking alcohol and smoking marijuana all day leading up to the incident. The subject advised dispatch that he wanted to attack a police officer with a knife and commit suicide by cop. The armed subject indicated that he was not willing to come out of his residence and that he would attack anyone that got near to him. He was determined to wait inside his residence until he was shot. Officers from neighboring jurisdictions, which would be Green Oak Township and Lyon Township, Oakland County, Set up a perimeter around the location and evacuated the surrounding residents. Sergeant Wintrock had Southline Fire and EMS staged near the front of the mobile home community. Officer Barber began a dialogue with the subject through the rear window And eventually talked him out of the residence and onto the porch. He exited the trailer waving a large knife. Several officers, while he had him at gunpoint, ordered the subject to drop his weapon. The subject's only response was, shoot me. me. During the commotion, Sergeant Whitrock was able to make his way to the Subject's location without being detected. He deployed his taser and Disarmed the subject. He was physically subdued without further incident and transported to the hospital for a mental health evaluation. Sergeant Whitrock and Officer Barber's actions and regard to safety of everyone involved undoubtedly Led to the positive outcome where no one was seriously hurt and the subject was Able to receive the medical attention that he needed and for that the award is A department citation that which reads in part the outstanding performance of a Difficult task involving personal risk to the officers safety when because the Officers actions a serious crime is prevented or that if a perpetrator is Apprehended so with that I would like to present them with the department
